361 BacDive strains classified here; the 200 listed are the type strains and the best-deposited, see data/raw/bacdive_strains.tsv for all.

NCBI assemblies appear first, followed by GTDB, BV-BRC/PATRIC and IMG genome records. Links retain evidence from BacDive, GTDB metadata and GOLD. Supplied accession versions, source strain identifiers and database meanings are preserved. NCBI accessions without a version suffix have unspecified version. Links under one strain do not establish genome equivalence; source descriptions may identify a substrain or plasmid. Samples and GOLD organism, sequencing-project and analysis-project records are listed separately. The complete NCBI assembly inventory, other genome-record inventory and related-record inventory include strains omitted from this listing.
